## 5. Vendor-published software

No package manager owns these. Provenance was recorded by hand in
`config/build-update/unmanaged-software.yaml`. Where no publisher or
repository could be identified, that is stated rather than guessed.

| Item | Publisher | Repository | **Pinned** | **Released** | Match | Download | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `QGroundControl-x86_64.AppImage` | - | mavlink/qgroundcontrol | `no version in filename` | `v5.1.5` | yes | [link](https://github.com/mavlink/qgroundcontrol/releases/download/v5.1.5/QGroundControl-x86_64.AppImage) | CHECKED via the releases API.
